TL;DR: 本研究提出渐进经验融合（PEF）方法训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器，用于自主血管内导航（机械取栓术）。在10个已知训练解剖结构中，PEF平均成功率达74%，优于SAC（37%）和基础TD-MPC2（65%）；在30个血管系统上训练并采用自适应规划窗口后，在10个未见血管系统中达到90%成功率，并成功迁移到体外卒中患者血管模型（平均路径比从63%提升至80%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主血管内导航有望将机械取栓术推广到医疗资源匮乏地区，但控制器需要跨越不同血管解剖结构的多个长路径阶段，现有方法难以应对多血管、多任务的复杂导航需求。

Method: 1. 提出渐进经验融合（PEF）用于训练多任务TD-MPC2控制器；2. 设计基于残差动作序列离散度的自适应规划窗口启发式方法，动态调整MPPI规划水平；3. 在患者特异性模拟中进行微调。

Result: PEF在10个已知训练解剖结构达成74%平均成功率（vs SAC 37%, p<0.001; vs 基础TD-MPC2 65%, p=0.053）；在30个血管上训练并加自适应规划后，10个未见血管系统成功率达90%；体外卒中患者血管模型下成功转移，微调后平均路径比从63%提升至80%（p<0.001），约需40×10³微调步骤（约107分钟院内转运时间）。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了多血管训练与患者特异性适应的概念可行性，为自主机械取栓导航提供了有前景的方向，但仍需进一步临床验证才能部署。

Abstract: Autonomous endovascular navigation could support the delivery of mechanical thrombectomy to underserved areas, but controllers must navigate long, multi-stage paths across varying vascular anatomies. This study investigates Progressive Experience Fusion (PEF) to train a multi-task TD-MPC2 controller. We additionally evaluate a heuristic that changes the Model Predictive Path Integral planning horizon using residual action-sequence dispersion, and fine-tuning in a patient-specific simulation. Across five subtasks in ten known training anatomies with held-out targets, PEF achieved a mean success rate of 74%, compared with 37% for Soft Actor-Critic (p < 0.001) and 65% for base TD-MPC2 (p = 0.053). A PEF controller with adaptive-horizon planning trained on 30 vasculatures achieved a mean success rate of 90% in ten held-out vasculatures. The PEF agent successfully transferred to an unseen in vitro stroke patient vasculature under fluoroscopy, achieving a mean path ratio improvement from 63% to 80% with fine-tuning (p < 0.001), following 40x103 fine-tuning steps (corresponding to approximately 107 min of clinical inter-hospital transfer time). This work represents a proof of concept for multi-vasculature training and patient-specific adaptation, while further validation is required before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [102] [Orienteering Problem with Uncertain Time-Varying Rewards: Framework and Benchmark for Everyday Service Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18672)
*Masafumi Endo,Kohei Honda,Yuu Jinnai,Ryo Yonetani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了带不确定时变奖励的定向运动问题（OP-UTVR），允许智能体从观测中估计奖励动态并预测未来奖励，通过三种不同规划水平与在线自适应能力的规划器加以解决，并引入了移动服务机器人的基准测试，实验展示了规划视野与自适应性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有定向运动问题（OP）大多假设奖励在规划前已知，但实际应用中奖励往往是不确定且随时间变化的（如交付代理面临的客户需求变化），需要放宽此假设以支撑更实际的路径决策。

Method: 提出OP-UTVR变体，允许智能体从观测中估计奖励动态并预测未来奖励；设计三种在规划视野与在线自适应性上不同的规划器；推导在奖励随机性下的理论性能界；构建移动服务机器人在室内环境行人中导航的基准测试。

Result: 实验揭示了规划视野与自适应性之间的权衡，并展示了结合在线自适应的长视野规划的有效性。

Conclusion: 长视野规划配合在线自适应是处理不确定时变奖励的有效策略，为OP在实际动态场景中的应用提供了理论依据与实证支持。

Abstract: We present the orienteering problem with uncertain time-varying rewards (OP-UTVR), a novel variant of the orienteering problem (OP). While most existing OP formulations assume rewards to be known in advance, practical applications involve uncertain and time-varying rewards, as with shifting customer demand for delivery agents. OP-UTVR relaxes this assumption by allowing agents to estimate reward dynamics from observations and forecast future rewards. This enables informed routing decisions despite stochastic reward changes and inevitable prediction errors. We address this problem using three planners that differ in planning horizon and online adaptivity, and derive theoretical bounds on their performance under reward stochasticity. We further introduce a mobile service robot benchmark for OP-UTVR, where a robot navigates among pedestrians in indoor environments. Experiments reveal trade-offs between planning horizon and adaptivity, and demonstrate the effectiveness of long-horizon planning with online adaptation.

</details>


### [103] [SoftVTBench: A Deformation-Aware Visuo-Tactile Dataset and Benchmark for Deformable-Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18701)
*Bowen Jing,Mingxin Wang,Ruiyang Hao,Chenchen Ge,Hanwen Shen,Junjie He,Yang Cui,Yiming Hou,Weitao Zhou,Jiawei Wang,Minglei Li,Dandan Zhang,Ding Zhao,Houde Liu,Xiaofan Li,Si Liu,Ping Luo,Haibao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftVTBench是一个面向物理交互感知的可变形物体操作视觉-触觉数据集，包含4000个专家演示和50多个资产，同步多视角RGB、双指触觉、本体感觉、语言及有限元状态。基于此建立的闭环基准引入形变感知成功率(DSR)，要求任务成功且峰值归一化形变在容差内。实验发现所有12个分布内配置中均存在违反形变容差但任务成功的rollout，且触觉可用并不自动保证有效的多模态融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体操作基准仅评估任务成功率，忽略物理交互质量（如滑移、过度压缩）。瓶颈在于缺乏将策略可见的接触观测与独立物理真值配对的视觉-触觉数据集。

Method: 构建SoftVTBench数据集（4,000专家演示、50+资产、20Hz同步多模态数据），并建立基于固定对象特定标定的闭环基准，定义形变感知成功率(DSR)作为评估指标。

Result: 在Diffusion Policy、π0.5、FastWAM上，所有12个分布内配置都有违反形变容差的成功rollout（占各配置成功率的0.7-24%）。分布偏移下，视觉-触觉变体在全部6个对比中任务成功率更高，5个对比中DSR更高，但分布内收益不一。

Conclusion: 研究证明提供触觉信息本身并不能确保有效的多模态融合。SoftVTBench为研究策略不仅是否成功、还如何与可变形物体物理交互以及触觉何时改善交互提供了共享资源。

Abstract: Physical interaction quality is central to deformable-object manipulation, yet most benchmarks evaluate task success alone. A policy may complete the task while allowing slip or causing excessive compression. A primary bottleneck is the absence of visuo-tactile datasets that pair policy-visible contact observations with independent physical ground truth over complete tasks. We introduce SoftVTBench, a visuo-tactile dataset for physical-interaction-aware deformable-object manipulation. It contains 4,000 expert demonstrations and more than 50 assets, including volumetric deformable objects and visually matched rigid twins. At 20 Hz, each episode synchronizes multi-view RGB, dual-finger tactile RGB and marker motion, proprioception, language, and binary and continuous gripper actions, alongside evaluator-only finite-element (FEM) states. Building upon this dataset, we establish a closed-loop benchmark that uses fixed object-specific calibration to define the Deformation-aware Success Rate (DSR), which counts a rollout as successful only when it completes the task and keeps peak normalized deformation within tolerance. Across Diffusion Policy, $π_{0.5}$, and FastWAM, all 12 in-distribution configurations contain successful rollouts that violate the deformation tolerance, accounting for 0.7--24% of each configuration's successes. Under distribution shift, visuo-tactile variants achieve higher task success in all six policy--suite comparisons and higher DSR in five, whereas their in-distribution benefits are mixed. These results show that making touch available does not by itself ensure effective multimodal fusion. SoftVTBench therefore provides a common visuo-tactile resource for studying not only whether a policy succeeds, but how it physically interacts with deformable objects and when touch improves that interaction.

</details>


### [104] [Dream2Reward: Transition-Alignment Reward Models from Positive Demonstrations for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18787)
*Haoyu Zhang,Zecui Zeng,Bin Wang,Lusong Li,Liang Lin,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream2Reward通过从正样本演示中学习语言条件化的成功潜态转移场，将实现的动作与预测的成功变化进行比较，从而生成密集因果奖励，无需失败标注或进步标签，在在线和离线策略学习中均优于基于进步的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习机器人策略需要密集奖励，但基于进步估计的奖励在错误转换后可能仍然保持高值，无法有效区分低质量行为。现有方法通常需要失败标注或进步标签，成本高昂且难以获取。

Method: Dream2Reward学习一个语言条件化的成功潜态转移场：给定视觉历史，预测与成功执行相关的潜位移，并通过有符号方向一致性和对称幅度一致性评估实际观测到的位移。该方法在转换级别进行比较，能够惩罚错误方向、过度执行和停滞运动。

Result: 在机制诊断和共享轨迹评估中，Dream2Reward比基于进步的替代方法提供更强的成功失败区分能力和更丰富的低质量行为反馈。在在线和离线策略学习中，冻结的奖励模型减少了奖励黑客行为并支持更强的下游性能，包括真实机器人操作。

Conclusion: 将实现的动作与预测的成功变化进行比较，是将正样本演示转化为机器人学习密集奖励的有效方式。Dream2Reward无需失败标注、进步标签或合成负样本，即可产生密集因果奖励。

Abstract: Learning robotic policies requires dense rewards that remain informative when behavior departs from successful demonstrations. Progress-based rewards estimate how far an observation has advanced along a nominal successful trajectory, but may remain high after an incorrect transition. We introduce Dream2Reward, which learns a language-conditioned successful latent transition field from positive demonstrations. Given the visual history up to a transition start, the model predicts the latent displacement associated with successful execution and scores the observed displacement through signed directional and symmetric magnitude agreement. This transition-level comparison penalizes wrong-direction, overshooting, and stagnant motion even when the resulting observation appears to show progress. Dream2Reward requires no failure annotations, progress labels, or synthetic negatives, and produces a dense causal reward. Across mechanism diagnostics and shared-trajectory evaluations, it provides stronger success-failure separation and more informative feedback on low-quality behavior than progress-based alternatives. Across online and offline policy learning, the same frozen reward model reduces reward hacking and supports stronger downstream performance, including in real-robot manipulation. These results show that comparing realized motion with predicted successful change provides an effective way to convert positive demonstrations into dense rewards for robot learning.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Placement and Articulation: Usage-Driven Code Scenes for Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18840)
*Zijian Xiao,Zipeng Ye,Jinkun Hao,Xiong Yang,Yuchen Xie,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoomWright是一个智能体驱动的室内场景合成框架，将3D场景完全以代码形式表示为具身交互环境。它通过使用驱动的对象推理、多部分交互编译（将交互转化为触发-条件-效果规则）以及标注引导的朝向优化，生成可执行、可编辑、可仿真的交互式场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码的场景生成方法虽然能生成可编辑和可扩展的环境，但仅关注视觉构建和物体级关节运动，忽略了场景的功能性使用（functional usage），导致场景缺乏可交互的具身交互能力。

Method: 1) 使用驱动的对象推理（usage-driven object reasoning）：将每个锚点视为任务中心，接纳任务所需物体及其功能属性；2) 代码智能体实现多部分交互：将每个交互编译为"触发-条件-效果"（trigger, condition, effect）规则，更新结构化对象状态，捕捉物体间的因果依赖；3) 标注引导的使用导向朝向优化（annotation-informed usage-guided orientation）：解决操作物体朝向从像素难以恢复的歧义问题。

Result: 大量实验证明了该方法在生成可执行、可编辑、仿真就绪的交互式场景方面的有效性，为具身AI和策略学习提供了交互环境。

Conclusion: RoomWright将场景生成从"看得见"推进到"用得上"，通过代码化的场景表示和交互规则编译，弥合了场景视觉构建与功能使用之间的鸿沟，为具身AI和仿真策略学习提供了更真实的交互环境。

Abstract: Indoor scene synthesis provides essential environments for embodied AI, robotic manipulation, and simulation-based policy learning. Recent code-based scene generation methods produce editable and extensible environments, yet they remain focused on visual construction and object-level articulation, leaving the functional usage of scenes largely unmodeled. To address this problem, we present RoomWright, an agentic usage-driven framework for generating 3D scenes represented entirely as code for embodied interaction. RoomWright performs usage-driven object reasoning, which treats each anchor as a task centre and admits task-required objects and their affordances. A code agent further enables multi-part interaction by compiling each interaction into a trigger, condition, effect rule that updates structured object states, capturing causal dependencies across objects. Moreover, since manipuland orientation is ambiguous and hard to recover from pixels, RoomWright alleviates this via annotation-informed usage-guided orientation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. The resulting scenes are executable, editable, and simulation-ready, providing interactive environments for embodied AI and policy learning.

</details>


### [106] [RoboEdit: Turning Human Manipulation Videos into Scalable Robot Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18948)
*Yaowei Guo,Zeng Tao,Yuxin Jiang,Yunuo Chen,Zhiyang Dou,Yuxiang Ma,Yin Yang,Demetri Terzopoulos,Ying Jiang,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboEdit是一个将人类操作视频转换为机器人视频的编辑套件，包含自动管线RoboEdit-ADC（用于3D交互重建与重定向）、大规模数据集RoboEdit-14M（174K配对视频，1400万帧，覆盖7种机器人形态）以及核心编辑引擎RoboEdit-Trans（跨形态适应模块+3D机器人状态解码器），在编辑质量和下游真实世界机器人控制策略上均达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手-物交互数据的采集成本高昂且受限于特定机器人形态，而大量现成的人类操作视频无法直接用于机器人训练，亟需一种能利用海量人类视频进行可扩展机器人学习的方法。

Method: 提出RoboEdit视频编辑套件：(1) RoboEdit-ADC自动管线，从RGB视频重建3D交互并跨形态重定向；(2) 生成RoboEdit-14M大规模配对数据集；(3) RoboEdit-Trans编辑引擎，采用跨形态适应模块保持时间连贯性并适配外观和运动，集成3D机器人状态解码器恢复逐帧手部状态以提供结构化运动监督。

Result: 实验表明RoboEdit在编辑质量上达到最先进水平，并能有效支持真实世界操作任务中的下游机器人控制策略。

Conclusion: RoboEdit套件解锁了未标注人类视频的巨大潜力，为通用机器人学习提供了可扩展、高保真的视觉和3D运动监督。

Abstract: Collecting robot hand-object interaction data is costly and embodiment-specific, yet abundant human-object videos remain unusable for robot training. We present RoboEdit, a human-to-robot video editing suite that transforms human manipulation videos into action-consistent, physically plausible robot videos with aligned 3D hand states. To enable scalable supervision, we introduce RoboEdit-ADC, an automatic pipeline that reconstructs and retargets 3D interactions from RGB videos across embodiments. This pipeline generates RoboEdit-14M, a large-scale dataset of 174K aligned video pairs (14M frames) spanning seven robot embodiments, diverse scenes, and interaction types. The core editing engine, RoboEdit-Trans, employs cross-embodiment adaptation modules to preserve temporal coherence while adapting appearance and motion. It further integrates a 3D Robot-State Decoder to recover per-frame hand states for structured motion supervision. Experiments show that RoboEdit achieves state-of-the-art editing quality and supports downstream robot control policies in real-world manipulation tasks. Ultimately, the RoboEdit suite unlocks the vast potential of unlabeled human videos, providing scalable, high-fidelity visual and 3D motion supervision for generalizable robot learning.

</details>


### [107] [Autonomous Agricultural Tractor: Integrated Weed Detection and LiDAR Navigation for Precision Paddy Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19004)
*Benjamin Merryman-Smith,Tony Nguyen,Bilal Dogutas,Krish Shah,Anthony Raphael,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgriNav，一个集成的自主拖拉机系统，用于稻田定点杂草管理。系统由四个ROS耦合模块组成：基于PyTorch的WeedDet水稻检测、轻量级CNN-FPN杂草检测器（带非对称类权重）、倒置逻辑判别模块（硬编码置信门控保护水稻类），以及融合GNSS/IMU/轮速计的多状态扩展卡尔曼滤波器。核心贡献是四机制LiDAR-相机融合桥，利用导航LiDAR实现ROI约束、世界坐标投影、地面平面过滤和双向置信融合，无需额外硬件。模拟实验证明在20秒GNSS中断下仍能持续定位跟踪，作物行检测置信度保持在0.9以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稻田定点杂草管理虽能大幅减少除草剂使用，但实际部署面临三大挑战：GNSS在冠层下退化导致作物行导航不稳定、水稻与形态多样的杂草的实时视觉区分困难、以及将水稻误判为杂草的不可逆高成本问题。

Method: AgriNav系统由四个ROS耦合模块组成：(1)自定义PyTorch实现的WeedDet用于水稻检测；(2)轻量级168万参数的CNN-FPN变体，采用非对称类权重；(3)倒置逻辑判别模块，通过硬编码置信门控否决来保护水稻类别；以及(4)六状态恒定速度转弯率扩展卡尔曼滤波器，融合GNSS、IMU和轮式里程计，支持三级中断桥接。核心创新是四机制LiDAR-相机融合桥，利用导航LiDAR进行ROI约束、世界坐标投影、地面平面过滤和双向置信融合。

Result: 模拟实验表明：系统能在20秒GNSS中断期间持续定位跟踪；作物行检测置信度全程保持在0.9以上；水稻检测置信度在稻田、航拍和洪水后图像中达到0.32至0.95；LiDAR ROI约束将检测推理区域估计减少30-50%。

Conclusion: AgriNav通过多传感器融合和类保护机制，在零额外硬件成本下解决了稻田自主导航和杂草识别的关键挑战，证明了LiDAR-相机融合桥作为系统级方案的有效性，为稻田定点杂草管理的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Site-specific weed management in paddy farming offers substantial reductions in herbicide use over conventional broadcast spraying, but field deployment has been limited by three persistent challenges: robust crop-row navigation under canopy where GNSS degrades, real-time visual discrimination between rice and morphologically diverse weeds, and the asymmetric cost of misclassifying rice as weed, which is irreversible.
  This paper presents AgriNav, an integrated autonomous tractor system built around four ROS-coupled modules: a custom PyTorch reimplementation of WeedDet for rice detection, a parallel lightweight 1.68M-parameter CNN-FPN variant with asymmetric class weighting, an inverted-logic discrimination module that protects the rice class through a hardcoded confidence-gate veto, and a 6-state constant-velocity-turn-rate Extended Kalman Filter fusing GNSS, IMU, and wheel odometry with three-level outage bridging. Our primary system-level contribution is a four-mechanism LiDAR-camera fusion bridge that uses the navigation LiDAR for region-of-interest constraint, world-coordinate projection, ground-plane filtering, and bidirectional confidence fusion at zero additional hardware cost.
  Simulation experiments demonstrate continuous position tracking through a 20-second GNSS outage, crop row detection confidence above 0.9 throughout operation, and rice-detection confidences from 0.32 to 0.95 across paddy, aerial, and post-flood imagery. The LiDAR ROI constraint reduces detection inference region by an estimated 30 to 50 percent.

</details>


### [108] [LT-Mem: Volatility-Aware Spatio-Temporal Memory for Lifelong Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19059)
*Yumin Lee,Hyoseok Ju,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了LT-Mem框架，解决长期机器人操作中物体历史记忆丢失（时间遗忘）的问题。通过多会话SLAM提供空间对齐的逐物体观测，结合波动感知策略（覆盖/保持/多假设）管理记忆演化，并引入Tri-Memory结构（Live、Delta、Meta）保留当前状态与事件历史，支持跨会话的物体中心推理。同时提出LT-VQA数据集用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人建图系统要么直接覆盖历史以维护最新地图，要么存储语义快照但缺乏跨会话的物体身份一致性，导致"时间遗忘"问题——无法回答如"绿色椅子在所有会话中曾在哪些位置"这类需要物体历史信息的查询。

Method: 1) 多会话SLAM骨干：提供跨会话空间对齐的逐物体观测；2) 推理层：确定性证据评分保持跨会话身份，波动感知策略根据物体动态性在覆盖、保持、多假设三种动作中选择；3) Tri-Memory结构（Live/Delta/Meta）同时保存当前状态和事件历史；4) 提出LT-VQA数据集用于评估。

Result: LT-Mem在所有指标上一致优于基线方法，且消耗的token数量少一个数量级；消融实验证实性能提升来源于结构化记忆架构而非LLM容量。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆架构是解决长期机器人操作中物体级时间遗忘问题的关键，高效且有效。

Abstract: Long-term robot operation in evolving environments requires object-level understanding that persists across repeated revisits. Existing systems either overwrite history to maintain an up-to-date map or store semantic snapshots without consistent cross-session object identity, resulting in temporal amnesia: the systematic loss of object history that prevents answering queries such as "Where has the green chair been across all sessions?" We propose LT-Mem, a volatility-aware memory evolution framework that unifies spatially aligned instance-level 3D perception with volatility-conditioned temporal reasoning. First, a multi-session SLAM backbone provides spatially aligned per-object observations across sessions. Second, a reasoning layer governs how object memory evolves: deterministic evidence scoring preserves cross-session identity, and a volatility-aware policy selects among overwrite, hold, and multi-hypothesis actions based on each object's dynamics. Third, the resulting Tri-Memory structure (Live, Delta, Meta) preserves both current states and event histories, enabling longitudinal object-centric reasoning. We further introduce LT-VQA, a dataset and evaluation suite comprising multi-session recordings, persistent identity annotations, and temporal QA pairs. Experiments show that LT-Mem consistently outperforms baselines across all metrics while consuming an order of magnitude fewer tokens, and ablations confirm that gains are driven by the structured memory architecture rather than LLM capacity.

</details>


### [109] [DA-WAM: Decision-Aligned Future Latents for Driving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19085)
*Ruiguo Zhong,Benshan Ma,Xiaolong Chen,Lang Zhang,Mingyue Feng,Yaonong Wang,Pei Liu,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-WAM框架，统一了预测性表示学习、动作条件未来建模和轨迹评分于单一决策目标下，通过在线编码器和稳定动量目标维持预测监督，使未来表示与驾驶任务共同演化，并在NAVSIM基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型存在关键缺陷：未来建模仅具预测性而缺乏决策信息性——预测的未来不能直接指导轨迹选择。已有方法要么将未来表示学习与规划优化解耦，要么在不同轨迹候选间共享预测状态，稀释了应当指导选择的行为特定后果。

Method: DA-WAM包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过在线编码器和稳定动量目标，在规划优化全程维持预测监督，使未来表示与驾驶任务共同演化；(2) 动作条件预测器为每条轨迹候选生成独立的未来潜在状态；(3) 未来潜在状态条件化的因子化打分器评估各轨迹。专家匹配轨迹的未来潜在状态由观测未来表示监督，并利用安全关键硬负样本在规划边界附近提供额外监督。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2上取得最先进性能，消融实验和诊断分析验证了各关键组件的有效性。

Conclusion: DA-WAM证明了将未来建模与决策优化统一于单一目标下，能够使世界模型的预测真正服务于轨迹选择，为自动驾驶决策提供更具信息价值的未来表示。

Abstract: Anticipating how scenes evolve under ego actions is fundamental to safe autonomous driving, yet the full potential of world models for decision-making remains unrealized. The critical challenge lies in ensuring that future modeling is not merely predictive, but decision-informative: the predicted future must directly shape which trajectory is selected. Existing approaches decouple future representation learning from planning optimization, or share predicted states across trajectory candidates, thereby diluting the action-specific consequences that ought to guide selection. To bridge this gap, we propose DA-WAM, a framework that unifies predictive representation learning, action-conditioned future modeling, and trajectory scoring under a single decision-making objective. DA-WAM maintains predictive supervision throughout planner optimization via an online encoder and a stable momentum target, allowing future representations to co-evolve with the driving task. An action-conditioned predictor generates a distinct future latent state per trajectory candidate, which is then evaluated by a future-latent-conditioned factorized scorer. For the expert-matched trajectory, the predicted future latent is supervised by the observed future representation, while safety-critical hard negatives provide additional supervision near planning boundaries. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, while ablations and diagnostic analyses validate the key components.

</details>


### [110] [ADEPT: Accelerating Dexterity via Pre-Training and Post-Training using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19182)
*Jayjun Lee,Jessica Yin,Asif Rana,Nicholas Blauch,Sam Mady,Mohak Bhardwaj,Nima Fazeli,Nathan Ratliff,Karl Van Wyk,Ankur Handa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ADEPT，一个大规模强化学习框架，通过在通用物体重定位任务上预训练灵巧策略，再针对下游任务进行后训练，实现了高自由度多指机器人的sim-to-real迁移。该框架结合行为克隆蒸馏、critic预热和保守on-policy更新，配合关节空间Geometric Fabric，在两个高自由度机器人平台上实现了从原始视觉触觉感知出发的长时间跨度任务，达到人类水平的灵巧操作速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由度多指机器人的灵巧操作任务难以从头学习，且每个新下游任务都需要重新学习相同的技能，缺乏可迁移的预训练基础。同时，从原始视觉触觉感知直接学习长时间跨度任务具有巨大挑战。

Method: ADEPT框架包含三个阶段：(1) 在通用物体重定位任务上大规模预训练灵巧策略作为先验；(2) 通过稳定的后训练配方（行为克隆蒸馏、critic预热、保守on-policy更新）将预训练行为转化为下游任务策略，避免naïve RL微调导致的退化；(3) 引入关节空间Geometric Fabric作为RL策略与机器人之间的中介，安全利用完整运动学灵巧性，并将教师策略蒸馏为基于感知的学生策略实现zero-shot sim-to-real迁移。

Result: 在23 DoF的Kuka-Allegro（配备两个RGB相机）和29 DoF的Flexiv-Sharpa（配备两个RGB相机和五个视觉触觉传感器）两个平台上实现了zero-shot sim-to-real迁移，能够从具有挑战性的初始状态解决长时间跨度任务，并以人类水平的速度完成灵巧操作。

Conclusion: ADEPT证明了大规模预训练可以显著加速多指机器人的灵巧技能学习，通过精心设计的后训练策略避免预训练能力的退化，并借助Geometric Fabric和蒸馏技术实现了从仿真到真实世界的可靠迁移，为通用灵巧操作提供了一个可扩展的学习框架。

Abstract: We introduce Accelerating Dexterity via Pre-Training (ADEPT), a large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) framework for learning sim-to-real transferable dexterity across high degree-of-freedom (DoF) robot embodiments that can solve long-horizon tasks directly from raw visuo-tactile perception. ADEPT pretrains a dexterous policy on a generic object reposing task, then post-trains downstream policies with this pretrained behavior as a prior. ADEPT enables learning new behaviors that are otherwise difficult to discover from scratch on multi-fingered robots and avoids learning the same set of skills over again for every new downstream task. The pretrained policy zero-shots the reposing phase of downstream tasks, but naïve RL fine-tuning rapidly degrades this capability during transfer. We address this with a stable post-training recipe combining behavior-cloning distillation, critic warm-up, and conservative on-policy updates. To safely exploit the full kinematic dexterity, we introduce a joint-space Geometric Fabric that mediates between the RL policy and the robot. We distill post-trained teachers into perceptive students that zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on two embodiments: a 23 DoF Kuka-Allegro with two RGB cameras, and a 29 DoF Flexiv-Sharpa with two RGB cameras and five vision-based tactile sensors, and can solve long-horizon tasks from challenging initial states with dexterity at human-level speed.

</details>


### [111] [PartialBiGrasp: Inferring Hidden Local Geometry for Bimanual Grasping from Partial Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19188)
*Ayush Kaura,Vignesh Vembar,Md Faizal Karim,Keshab Patra,K Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PartialBiGrasp，一种直接基于部分点云观测的双臂抓取生成框架，无需完整物体点云，通过卷积占用网络隐式学习几何特征（可抓取性、无碰撞接触区域、物体厚度），生成力闭合抓取对并用基于采样的优化进行精炼，在解析力闭合指标、大规模仿真和真实机器人实验中均验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂抓取对大型、重型、几何复杂物体至关重要，但现有双臂抓取方法假设能获得物体的完整点云，这在真实场景中往往不可行。实际观测到的通常是部分点云，因此需要开发能直接从不完整几何信息中进行双臂抓取生成的方法。

Method: 1) 构建PartialBiGrasp框架，直接处理部分点云观测；2) 使用卷积占用网络（convolutional occupancy networks）隐式学习几何特征，实现对可抓取性、无碰撞接触区域和物体厚度的局部推理；3) 基于学到的几何理解生成力闭合（force-closure）抓取对；4) 采用基于采样的优化方法对抓取对进行精炼，修正因几何不完整导致的抓取歧义。

Result: 通过解析力闭合指标评估、大规模仿真实验以及真实机器人实验，在包含噪声的部分点云和新颖物体上验证了该方法能够生成鲁棒且物理稳定的双臂抓取。

Conclusion: PartialBiGrasp证明了即使在仅有部分点云观测的不完整信息条件下，也能可靠地生成双臂力闭合抓取，为真实场景中双臂机器人操作大型物体提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Dual-arm robotic grasping is essential for manipulating large, heavy, and geometrically complex objects that cannot be reliably handled using a single manipulator. These large objects often contain only sparse graspable regions determined by local geometric properties such as thickness, edge structure, and gripper clearance. Prior bimanual grasping methods assume access to a full point cloud of the object which inherently contains this geometric information, but may not be accessible in real scenarios. This work proposes PartialBiGrasp, a dual-arm grasp generation framework that operates directly on partial point cloud observations. Our model learns geometric features implicitly through convolutional occupancy networks, enabling local reasoning about graspability, collision-free contact regions, and object thickness. We leverage this understanding to generate force-closure compliant grasp pairs, which are further refined using a sampling-based optimization to correct for ambiguity caused by incomplete geometry. We evaluate our approach using analytical force-closure metrics, large-scale simulation experiments, and real-world robot evaluations on noisy partial point clouds of novel objects, demonstrating robust and physically stable dual-arm grasp generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个立场：具有链式推理能力的AI智能体天生倾向于产生合谋行为，在影响经济市场决策之前必须获得行为认证。作者通过DeepSeek-R1在伯特兰寡头定价领域的实验证实了这一倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将推理型AI智能体整合入社会可能导致法律上区分竞争与合谋的证据标准崩溃（尽管经济危害区分仍然存在），因为链式推理可以被引导到合谋或竞争方向，且这种引导在语义上无法被检测，导致市场中出现合谋的经济结果却没有任何共谋或意图的证据。

Method: 使用DeepSeek-R1智能体在伯特兰寡头定价领域进行实验，测试其在无人提示干预下的合谋倾向、人类提示不要合谋时的行为，以及通过链式推理引导智能体向合谋或竞争方向发展的可检测性。同时检验这类智能体是否可被引导至高效竞争均衡。

Result: 实验发现：（1）DeepSeek-R1智能体表现出持续性的默契合谋倾向，即使人类提示不要合谋也无法消除；（2）链式推理可被引导至极端合谋或高度竞争行为，且另一个LLM分析推理轨迹时无法在语义上检测出这种操纵；（3）初步证据表明智能体可以被概括性地引导向高效竞争均衡。

Conclusion: 基于代表性情境中观察到的行为认证对于防止AI合谋是必要的。在开发出全面的行为认证体系之前，这些模型不能被部署到真实市场，否则将威胁市场的稳定性与效率。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI agents with chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities are predisposed to exhibit collusive behavior and should be required to obtain behavioral certification before making decisions that affect economic markets. This is because integrating these agents into society could collapse the legal evidentiary distinction between competition and collusion among independent firms without eroding the economic harm distinction. Experiments with DeepSeek-R1 agents in the Bertrand oligopoly pricing domain reveal a tendency towards tacit collusion that persists even when humans prompt the agents not to collude. We further show that the chain-of-thought of these agents can be steered toward either extremely collusive or highly competitive behavior in a way that is not semantically detectable by another LLM analyzing the reasoning traces. As a result, deploying reasoning agents for market decisions leads to collusive economic outcomes without any evidence of conspiracy or intent. Thus, certification based on observed behavior in representative situations is necessary to prevent collusion. We provide preliminary evidence that such agents can be steered in a generalizable way toward efficient competitive equilibria. However, developing a comprehensive behavioral certification will be required before these models can be deployed in real-world markets while ensuring their stability and efficiency.

</details>


### [113] [Position: Profiling Game Worlds by Transition Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18079)
*Lele Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出过渡复杂度剖面(TCP)，一套可复现的度量集合，用于刻画游戏环境或游戏数据集的转移核复杂度，涵盖单步分支、交互不确定性和时空依赖跨度，并呼吁将其作为GWM与RL论文的标准基准元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏世界建模（GWM）与强化学习（RL）常被混淆，因为研究论文很少量化在声明的接口（像素/令牌/潜在表示及有限历史）下底层转移预测问题的难度，导致跨基准比较缺乏公平性。

Method: 提出Transition Complexity Profile (TCP)，包含三类指标：(i) 内在单步分支度；(ii) 交互引起的不确定性与可见时的对手影响；(iii) 通过标准化探针曲线衡量的时间/空间依赖跨度。TCP需配合显式参考分布、协议随机性和版本化测量预算（采样/重采样及固定探针算力）报告，以实现跨基准可比性。

Result: 论文概述了常见游戏家族和现代"神经游戏引擎"领域如何分布在该复杂度图谱中，展示了TCP作为基准对比依据的可行性。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁将TCP作为标准基准元数据，并成为GWM和RL论文中的必需统计数据，以解决领域内转移问题难度无法量化比较的根本问题。

Abstract: Game world modeling (GWM) and reinforcement learning (RL) are often confounded because research papers rarely quantify how difficult the underlying transition prediction problem is at the declared interface (pixels/tokens/latents with finite history). We propose the Transition Complexity Profile (TCP): a small, reproducible set of metrics that characterizes an environment's (or gameplay dataset's) induced transition kernel by (i) intrinsic one-step branching, (ii) interaction-induced uncertainty and opponent influence when observable, and (iii) temporal/spatial dependency span via standardized probe curves. TCP is reported with an explicit reference distribution, protocol stochasticity, and a versioned measurement budget (sampling/resampling and fixed probe compute), enabling comparable numbers across benchmarks. We outline how common game families and modern "neural game engine" domains populate this landscape and call for TCP to become standard benchmark metadata and a required statistic in GWM and RL papers.

</details>


### [114] [Large Language Models in Mental Health: A Systematic Review of Applications, Innovations, and Ethical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18080)
*Yisong Chen,Yifan Gao,Sijing Yu,Chuqing Zhao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型（LLMs）在心理健康领域的应用，涵盖社交媒体分析、临床对话代理、治疗支持、提示工程、多模态学习及伦理考量，并探讨了抑郁症早期检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持等方向，同时讨论了多模态融合技术和部署中的伦理与监管挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康问题日益严峻，传统诊疗资源有限，而大语言模型在自然语言理解和生成上的突破为心理健康筛查、诊断和支持提供了新的可能性。作者旨在系统梳理该领域跨学科研究成果，帮助研究者理解LLM在心理健康应用中的现状、机遇与挑战。

Method: 本文为综述性研究，通过整合跨学科研究，系统梳理了LLM在心理健康领域的主要应用场景（社交媒体分析、临床对话代理、治疗支持等），分析数据来源（社交媒体帖子、电子病历、多模态输入），总结模型与标注策略进展，探讨提示工程对领域适配的作用，并评述多模态融合技术及伦理监管框架。

Result: 综述发现LLM在抑郁症早期检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗及心理教育内容生成等方面取得显著进展；提示工程在多模态和临床领域适配中发挥关键作用；多模态融合（文本、语音、传感器数据）提升了诊断和监测能力；同时识别出伦理、社会技术及监管方面的持续挑战。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁建立安全、公平、负责任的框架，以推动LLM在真实世界心理健康护理中的可靠部署，强调在追求技术进步的同时必须重视伦理保障与制度规范。

Abstract: We present a review on the applications of large language models (LLMs) in health, e.g., social media analysis, clinical conversational agents, therapy support tools, prompt engineering, multimodal learning, and ethical considerations. We integrate findings from interdisciplinary studies utilizing diverse data sources such as social media posts, electronic medical records, and multimodal inputs to enable early detection of depression, suicide risk assessment, personalized therapy support, and psychoeducational content generation. Our review highlights advancements in LLM models and annotation strategies that enhance interpretability and clinical relevance, while we also emphasize the critical role of prompt engineering for domain adaptation. We also discuss emerging multimodal fusion techniques integrating text, speech, and sensor data for improved mental health diagnosis and monitoring. Finally, we address ongoing ethical, sociotechnical, and regulatory challenges, and advocate frameworks to ensure safe, equitable, and accountable deployment of LLMs in real-world mental health care.

</details>


### [115] [Position: Behavioral Systems Require Behavioral Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18081)
*Manuel Cherep,Nikhil Singh,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI代理系统应像其他行为系统一样被评估，即通过系统性的观察、扰动和行动解释来评估，而非仅关注性能结果，并提出了一套发展严格行为测试的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理系统的评估方法主要聚焦于性能结果（performance outcomes），而忽略了产生这些结果背后的行为过程。作者认为，鉴于AI代理日益作为行为系统运作（与动态环境交互、追求目标、随时间适应），仅评估结果是不够的，需要借鉴行为科学的方法来评估行为本身。

Method: 本文提出一个研究议程，包括三个核心方向：(1) 开发从行动序列中恢复决策策略的方法；(2) 构建能够隔离行为差异的环境；(3) 探测多代理系统中的涌现动态。这些方法借鉴行为科学的系统观察、扰动和解释框架。

Result: 本文是一个立场性/观点性论文，主要提出论点并勾勒研究议程，而非报告实证结果。其产出是一套行为测试方法论的路线图，旨在建立"AI行为科学"这一新领域。

Conclusion: 作者总结认为，这些研究方向共同为发展"AI行为科学"提供了路线图，强调将AI代理视为行为系统进行评估的重要性，为未来该领域的系统化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Artificial agentic systems increasingly operate as behavioral systems by interacting with dynamic environments, pursuing goals, and adapting over time. Yet, current evaluation methods largely focus on performance outcomes, not the underlying behavioral processes that produce them. This paper argues that AI agents must be evaluated like other behavioral systems: through systematic observation, perturbation, and interpretation of their actions. We draw on lessons from the behavioral sciences to motivate this position, and propose a research agenda focused on developing rigorous behavioral tests. These include methods for recovering decision strategies from action sequences, constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences, and probing emergent dynamics in multi-agent systems. Taken together, these directions offer a roadmap for developing a science of AI behavior.

</details>


### [116] [Position: Current Model Cards Are Insufficient for Downstream Governance of Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18086)
*Sungwon Chae,Keonwoo Kim,Hoki Kim,Jaeyeon Ju,Sangchul Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析Hugging Face上500个模型卡片，论证开放权重基础模型（OWFMs）的有效治理需要整合模型卡片、可接受使用政策（AUPs）和许可证三层互补组件，并指出现有治理框架存在的安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重基础模型（OWFMs）的快速增长带来了下游治理挑战，现有的模型卡片等透明度工具不足以充分告知下游开发者和用户关于OWFMs特有的安全挑战，需要重新评估治理策略。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），通过分析Hugging Face上500个模型卡片，从模型传承（heritage）、对齐溯源（alignment provenance）和经验观察行为三个维度识别现有监管方法留下的安全缺口，并论证标准开源许可证（OSLs）不适合OWFMs且可能削弱AUPs的可执行性。

Result: 发现现有治理框架存在安全缺口（包括模型传承、对齐溯源和经验观察行为方面），并提出将模型卡片、AUPs和许可证整合为综合性的安全工件，形成涵盖信息、规范和法定三个维度的全面治理框架。

Conclusion: 有效的OWFMs治理需要采用多层方法，将模型卡片、AUPs和许可证作为互补组件整合在一起，并使它们演变为集成的安全工件，从而构建更全面的治理框架。

Abstract: The growth of open-weight foundation models (OWFMs) has prompted the AI community to re-evaluate strategies for effective downstream governance. Although model cards have been widely adopted as transparency artifacts in model repositories, existing frameworks often fail to adequately inform downstream developers and users about the distinct safety challenges posed by OWFMs. This position paper analyzes 500 model cards hosted on Hugging Face and argues that effective governance of OWFMs requires a multi-layered approach integrating three complementary components: (i) model cards, (ii) acceptable use policies (AUPs), and (iii) licenses. To motivate this claim, we identify a safety gap left by existing regulatory approaches, including model heritage, alignment provenance, and empirically observed behaviors, through an analysis of model cards with safety-critical information. We further argue that standard open-source licenses (OSLs) are not well suited for OWFMs and may weaken the enforceability of AUPs. Building on these observations, we outline directions for evolving model cards, AUPs, and licenses into integrated safety artifacts to enable a more comprehensive governance framework that coherently integrates informational, normative, and legal dimensions.

</details>


### [117] [A Metamorphic Artificial Age Score Decision-Support Prototype for Flight-Log-Based Drone Propeller Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18088)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于变质人工年龄分数（AAS）的决策支持原型系统，用于无人机螺旋桨飞行日志健康监测。该系统利用2024 DronePropA公开数据集的历史真实飞行日志，计算六个健康相关指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定性、推力指令负担、电机指令不平衡等），并通过候选评分策略和变质充分性关系进行评估。回顾性评估表明，不同严重程度的螺旋桨缺陷通过不同操作通道表现，支持多指标决策支持层的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机螺旋桨故障的安全和可靠性风险往往分散在多个飞行日志通道中，而非呈现单一诊断信号，因此需要一种多指标融合的决策支持方法来检测和分类这些故障效应。

Method: 从原始MATLAB矩阵计算六个健康指标，基于健康基线进行归一化，通过候选评分策略、变质充分性关系和冗余调整AAS公式进行评估，采用受控回顾性评估方法：一个健康基线和三个缺陷螺旋桨案例，使用相同速度剖面和轨迹。

Result: 健康案例被分配到常规监测；严重度1案例以ESC指令不稳定为主，被分配到维护审查；严重度2案例达到电机指令和ESC指令负担最大值；严重度3案例达到最大轨迹跟踪误差，两者均触发强制检查。

Conclusion: 螺旋桨故障效应可能通过不同操作通道显现，支持在飞行后维护优先级排序和自主系统监督中采用多指标决策支持层的需求。

Abstract: Drone propeller faults can create safety and reliability risks when their effects are distributed across multiple flight-log channels rather than appearing as a single diagnostic signal. This paper proposes a Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS) decision-support prototype for flight-log-based drone propeller health monitoring. Using selected historical real flight logs from the 2024 DronePropA public dataset, the framework computes six health-related indicators from raw MATLAB matrices: trajectory tracking error, attitude instability, thrust-command burden, motor-command imbalance, ESC-command instability, and battery-level stress. These indicators are normalized relative to a healthy baseline and evaluated through candidate scoring policies, metamorphic adequacy relations, and a redundancy-adjusted AAS formulation. In this context, AAS is used as a structural policy-adequacy and burden measure rather than as a chronological age measure. A controlled retrospective evaluation was performed using one healthy baseline and three defective propeller cases under the same speed profile and trajectory. The healthy case was assigned to routine monitoring. The Severity 1 case was dominated by ESC-command instability and assigned to maintenance review. The Severity 2 case reached maximum motor-command and ESC-command burden, while the Severity 3 case reached maximum trajectory tracking error; both triggered mandatory inspection. The results show that propeller fault effects may appear through different operational channels, supporting the need for a multi-indicator decision-support layer for post-flight maintenance prioritization and autonomous-system oversight.

</details>


### [118] [Position: Multi-Agent Systems Should Prioritize Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18092)
*Xin Yang,Letian Li,Zimo Ji,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体系统（MAS）的许多失败本质上属于并发控制问题，而非传统的协调或通信问题。作者主张应将并发控制（冲突检测、隔离保证、共享资源的结构化访问）作为MAS框架的一等设计考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统虽然在理论上可扩展协作，但实际增加智能体数量反而导致可靠性下降。作者认为现有框架将失败归因于协调或通信问题，而忽略了这些失败实际上源于并发控制缺陷——智能体并发读写共享状态时，长推理窗口放大了脏读、丢失更新和不一致结果的风险。

Method: 这是一篇立场论文（position paper），采用论证分析的方法：将MAS中的常见失败模式映射到经典的并发异常（并发控制中的脏读、丢失更新等），并主张MAS框架应引入显式的并发控制机制，包括冲突检测、隔离保证和共享资源的结构化访问。

Result: 论文论证了MAS失败与经典并发控制异常之间的映射关系，并提出并发控制应作为MAS框架设计中的一等公民，而非事后补救措施。

Conclusion: MAS框架需要将并发控制作为核心设计原则，通过显式的冲突检测、隔离保证和结构化共享资源访问来提升系统可靠性，而非仅仅依赖协调或通信层面的改进。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) promise scalable collaboration, yet adding agents often reduces reliability. This position paper argues that many MAS failures are fundamentally concurrency control problems: agents concurrently read and write shared state, and long LLM inference windows amplify the risk of stale reads, lost updates, and inconsistent outcomes. Failure modes commonly attributed to coordination or communication breakdowns can be mapped directly onto classical concurrency anomalies. We contend that MAS frameworks should address these failures through explicit concurrency control mechanisms: conflict detection, isolation guarantees, and structured access to shared resources. Concurrency control should be a first-class design concern, not an afterthought.

</details>


### [119] [FinSkillBench: Evaluating AI Agents and Domain Skills for Investment Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18099)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinSkillBench，一个评估语言模型代理在投资管理领域使用金融技能能力的基准测试套件，涵盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三大领域共12个子任务和2,603个任务片段。研究发现精心策划的技能包能显著提升模型性能（平均分从0.366提升至0.528），而自生成技能收益甚微且计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资管理是高风险的决策领域，AI代理系统不能仅生成看似合理的文本，还需要检索时点数据、组装正确的计算输入、调用专业方法并产生可审计的结构化输出。然而，现有评估体系缺乏对语言模型代理能否有效使用金融领域专业技能的系统性衡量标准。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench基准，覆盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三大领域，含12个子任务和2,603个任务片段。每个片段提供时点输入、隐藏真实值和任务特定验证器。比较三种条件：无技能、策划技能包（程序性文档+可执行组件）、自生成技能（代理自主编写和复用程序）。使用9个模型进行大规模评估；并用独立框架（Hermes Agent，8个模型，5,280个片段）进行验证，以排除框架依赖。

Result: 策划技能包显著提升所有模型性能，平均分从0.366升至0.528，在投资组合构建和风险管理领域提升最大。相比之下，自生成技能收益甚微且计算成本更高。独立评估（Hermes Agent框架）在三大领域均复现了相同方向的结果，但技能效果的幅度随子任务和框架而变化。

Conclusion: 在投资管理代理中，访问可靠的程序性技能与模型选择同等重要，而朴素的自生成技能通常无效。研究发布基准、评估工具、策划技能包和完整轨迹以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Investment management is a high-stakes domain in which agentic AI systems must do more than generate plausible text. They must retrieve point-in-time data, assemble correct computational inputs, invoke specialized methods, and produce auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite designed to measure whether language model agents can effectively use financial domain skills to solve investment management tasks. The benchmark spans three domains, portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, and includes 12 subtasks with 2,603 task episodes.
  Each episode provides point-in-time inputs, hidden ground truth, and a task-specific verifier.We compare three conditions: no skill, curated skill packages consisting of procedural documents and executable components, and self-generated skills in which the agent writes and reuses its own procedures within an episode. Across 9 models and a large-scale evaluation, curated skills consistently improve performance, raising mean scores from 0.366 to 0.528, with the largest gains in portfolio construction and risk management.
  In contrast, self-generated skills provide little benefit despite higher computational cost. An independent evaluation using a separate agent framework (Hermes Agent, 8 models, 5,280 episodes total) reproduces the directional pattern across all three domains, with the magnitude of skill effects varying by subtask and harness.
  These results showthat in investment management agents, access to reliable procedural skills can be as important as model choice, while naive self-generation of skills is often ineffective. We release the benchmark, evaluation tools, curated skill packages, and full trajectories to support further research.

</details>


### [120] [Self-Evolving Agents as Dynamic Graph Transformation: A Survey and New Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18104)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Wenjie Zhang,Yin Chen,Xuemin Lin,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体演化视为动态图变换的框架，把智能体状态建模为动态图（记忆、工具、技能、工作流和智能体间关系作为类型化的节点、边和子图），并基于此组织四类动态图方法（节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活、跨组件协同演化），同时提出将动态图学习作为自演化智能体的可复用基础设施，并讨论图感知的评估与治理协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图-智能体综述通常将图视为智能体功能的支持结构，而非演化的基质；自演化智能体综述则聚焦于智能体层面的机制，很少讨论图拓扑演化。因此，演化中的智能体状态与动态图拓扑之间的耦合关系尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将智能体状态建模为动态图，其中记忆、工具、技能、工作流和智能体间关系被表示为类型化的节点、边和子图，通过模式约束的图重写进行更新。基于此形式化，将自演化智能体的动态图方法组织为四个分类：节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活和跨组件协同演化。进一步将九个动态图学习子领域映射到智能体演化能力，并讨论其适配与可能的失败模式。

Result: 提出了四类动态图方法分类体系，将九个动态图学习子领域映射到智能体演化能力，并从动态图视角讨论了五种图感知的评估与治理协议。

Conclusion: 本文的目标是为设计和治理自演化智能体提供一个紧凑的结构化视角，将智能体演化视为动态图变换，从而连接两条此前尚未充分交汇的研究路线。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly becoming self-evolving systems that persist across interactions, maintain memories, use tools, acquire skills, refine workflows, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make agent states structural and dynamic: entities, relations, attributes, dependencies, and execution structures change with new evidence, feedback, and environmental conditions. Existing graph-agent surveys typically treat graphs as support structures for agent functions rather than as evolving substrates, while self-evolving-agent surveys focus on agent-level mechanisms and rarely discuss graph topology evolution. Thus, the coupling between evolving agent state and dynamic graph topology remains underexplored. This survey connects these two research lines by framing \textit{agent evolution as dynamic graph transformation}. We model agent state as a dynamic graph, where memories, tools, skills, workflows, and inter-agent relations are represented as typed nodes, edges, and subgraphs updated through schema-constrained rewrites. Based on this formulation, we organize existing dynamic-graph-based methods for self-evolving agents into four taxonomies: node/feature evolution, edge/topology evolution, subgraph activation, and cross-component co-evolution. Building on this taxonomy, we propose dynamic graph learning as reusable infrastructure for self-evolving agents and map nine dynamic-graph-learning subfields to agent-evolution capabilities, discussing their adaptations and possible failure modes. Finally, we discuss five types of graph-aware evaluation and governance protocols from a dynamic-graph perspective, which complement end-task evaluation. The goal is to provide a compact structural lens for designing and governing self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [121] [Emergence of Agentic AI: A Review on Evolution, Background, Working Principles, Applications, Adoption Factors, and Future Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18110)
*AKM Bahalul Haque,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Mohammad Rayhan,Ivan Porres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于Agentic AI（智能体人工智能）的系统性综述，涵盖了该领域的基础原理、历史演进、架构、应用、挑战与未来研究方向，并提出了一个基于系统质量维度的利益相关者采纳框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI在人工智能领域快速发展，具有变革各领域的巨大潜力，但学界和业界对其理解仍不够深入。为了全面评估该技术的改进空间和应用前景，需要进行系统的综述研究，为研究者和从业者提供当前状态和未来研究方向的深入见解。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，收集近期各领域中有关Agentic AI的学术贡献，从多个维度进行分析：包括基本原理与工作机制、人工系统中agent性的历史与理论演进、架构与功能、各领域的实际应用、研究发现的综合分析、当前挑战识别及未来研究方向探讨，并基于提出的系统质量维度构建利益相关者采纳与使用框架。

Result: 完成了对Agentic AI的综合分析，明确了其当前发展现状和应用情况，识别出关键研究空白，概述了未来研究方向，并提出了一个包含系统质量维度的利益相关者采纳框架。

Conclusion: 本综述为研究者和从业者提供了对Agentic AI的全面理解，涵盖了其当前发展、应用、研究差距和未来方向，为该领域的进一步研究与实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agentic AI is gaining new insights and advancements in the field of Artificial Intelligence, fostering significant potential to enable rapid transformation across various domains.This rapid advancement and the potential to revolutionize various domains advocate the need for a deeper understanding and firm grasp of the technology. Moreover, an investigation into state of the art research directions in agentic AI needs to be conducted to comprehensively assess the potential scope for improvement and application.Therefore, to address these objectives, a comprehensive review can provide researchers and practitioners with valuable insights into the current state and future research scopes of agentic AI.Hence, this work considers the recently published scholarly contributions in agentic AI across various domains and discusses the fundamentals and working principles of Agentic AI, traces the historical and theoretical evolution of agency in artificial systems, explores and discusses Agentic AIs architecture, working principles, and functionalities, explores real-world applications of Agentic AI across various domains, analyzes the research findings, identifies current challenges, and discuss potential future research directions, and proposes a comprehensive framework of stakeholders intention to use and adopt Agentic AI with the help of proposed system quality dimensions.Therefore, this systematic review provides researchers and practitioners with a comprehensive understanding of Agentic AI, its current developments and applications, highlights key research gaps, and outlines future research directions.

</details>


### [122] [Solving Is Not Drawing: A Benchmark for Diagrammatic Reasoning in Olympiad Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18111)
*Hsien Xin Peng,Anthony Kim,Alvin Li,Calvin Supasanya,Shivank Garg,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源基准测试，包含954道奥林匹克几何题（其中297道为难题子集），每道题配有答案和人类作者的高保真Asymptote代码图，以及一整套文本、代码、图像、VLM和基于约束的指标来评估"图表推理"能力。实验发现当前基础模型在构图能力上远弱于解题能力，平均编译成功率仅36.14%，表明强数学推理能力并不等同于能构建准确的几何图形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的几何推理基准（如MathVista、MathVerse）只衡量模型能否得出正确答案，但没有一个基准单独隔离和衡量模型构建几何图形本身的能力。解题与作图是两种不同的技能，正确的推理不一定能产生忠实的图形，这一能力缺口尚未被测量。

Method: 构建了954道自包含的奥林匹克几何题数据集（含297道难题子集），每道题配有解答和人类撰写的高保真Asymptote代码图；设计了包括文本、代码、图像、VLM和基于约束的多种指标来评估图表推理能力。

Result: 评估当前基础模型发现解题与作图之间存在显著差距：模型的图表保真度明显不足，平均编译成功率仅为36.14%。强数学推理能力并不能保证构建准确几何图形的能力。

Conclusion: 几何问题求解与几何图形构建是两种独立的能力，现有基础模型在这两者之间存在明显鸿沟，因此有必要将图表推理作为独立能力进行专门评估和提升。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude now solve olympiad-level mathematics with remarkable proficiency, so much so that geometry problem solving has become a standard proxy for their mathematical reasoning. Yet solving a geometry problem and drawing the figure it depends on are not the same skill: progress often hinges on a faithful diagram with the right auxiliary constructions and incidences, and it is unclear that a model which reasons its way to the answer can also produce one. A growing collection of benchmarks, including MathVista, and MathVerse, measures whether models reach the correct answer, but to our knowledge, none isolate the distinct ability to construct the diagram itself, leaving this capability unmeasured. We introduce an open-source benchmark that targets this gap: 954 self-contained olympiad geometry problems, with a 297-problem hard subset, each paired with its solution and a human-authored, high-fidelity diagram in renderable Asymptote code, together with a suite of text-, code-, image-, VLM-, and constraint-based metrics for what we term diagrammatic reasoning. Evaluating current foundation models reveals a pronounced gap between solving and drawing: their diagrams are markedly less faithful, with an average compile success rate of only 36.14\%. Strong mathematical reasoning, we find, does not imply the ability to construct accurate geometric diagrams. Our benchmark and dataset can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/datasets/max98765/hard_geometry_problems_with_diagrams.

</details>


### [123] [Position: AI Leaderboards Are Underserving the Global South: A Case Study from India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18117)
*Sourav Banerjee,Saikat Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI排行榜在结构上不适配全球南方，问题不在于缺少高质量区域基准数据，而在于缺乏独立治理、利益冲突政策和指标演进机制。应以印度为案例研究，提出建立具备独立治理的区域性排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球南方的AI评估需求被忽视，现有全球排行榜缺乏治理机制纳入区域基准（如IndicSUPERB、MILU、LAHAJA、IrokoBench、AlGhafa），商业压力无法纠正影响全球南方用户的排行失败，问题长期存在却未得到解决。

Method: 采用立场论文形式，以印度（14亿人口、22种官方语言、已有高质量基准但缺乏可信聚合）为案例，并报告对58位AI从业者的咨询结果，显示他们一致偏好正式治理和基于披露的冲突管理。

Result: 58位AI从业者的咨询显示对正式治理和披露式冲突管理的一致偏好；证明了阻碍不在于数据缺失而在于制度设计；现有区域基准（印度、非洲、阿拉伯语）已具备但未被全球排行榜采纳。

Conclusion: 解决方案不是更多数据而是更好的制度：应从一开始就建立具备独立治理的区域性排行榜，而非依赖全球排行榜现有的无治理结构。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI leaderboards are structurally ill-suited to serving the Global South because they lack independent governance, conflict-of-interest policies, and mechanisms for metric evolution. The barrier is not missing data; high-quality regional benchmarks already exist: IndicSUPERB, MILU, and LAHAJA for India; IrokoBench for Africa; AlGhafa for Arabic. The barrier is institutional design. Global leaderboards do not include these benchmarks, and no governance mechanism compels them to do so. Commercial pressure corrects leaderboard failures when paying customers in the Global North are affected. The Global South lacks equivalent leverage. Without governance, failures affecting Hindi, Swahili, or Arabic speakers persist indefinitely as documented but unaddressed gaps. Using India as a case study (1.4 billion people, 22 scheduled languages, high-quality benchmarks, but no trusted aggregation), we report findings from a consultation with 58 AI practitioners showing consistent preference for formal governance and disclosure-based conflict management. The solution is not more data but better institutions: regional leaderboards with independent governance from the start.

</details>


### [124] [Safety Alignment Illusion: The Cross-Lingual Safety Gap in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18131)
*Namya Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出INCLUDE，一个针对印度文化社会偏见的跨语言多语评测基准，包含2,604个提示词、六种提示语言（英语、印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语、泰米尔语和兴都语混码），评测了十个开源和闭源LLM的14,988个偏见分数，发现孟加拉语在开源模型中偏见最高，而英语在开源模型中偏见最低但在闭源模型中偏见最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM的安全对齐训练严重以英语为中心，导致非英语语言的语音助手和对话系统可能绕过安全过滤，产生强化刻板印象的输出，对印度多语言群体传播有害偏见，这是一个关键失败模式，亟需跨语言偏见的评测工具。

Method: 构建INCLUDE多语评测基准，包含2,604个提示词，覆盖六种语言（英、印地、孟加拉、马拉地、泰米尔、兴都语混码），对十个开源和闭源LLM进行评测，生成14,988个偏见分数并进行统计分析。

Result: 两个关键发现：第一，孟加拉语在开源模型中产生最高的平均偏见分数；第二，英语呈现显著反转——在开源模型中偏见最低，但在闭源模型中偏见最高。

Conclusion: 跨语言安全对齐存在显著的不均衡性，现有的英语中心化安全训练无法有效覆盖印度多语言场景，需要针对非英语语言开发更全面的安全对齐机制。

Abstract: Current safety alignment training for Large Language Models (LLMs) are heavily English-centric. When such safety filters fail for non-English languages, the consequences are immediate and user-facing: voice assistants and spoken dialogue systems may produce stereotype-reinforcing outputs, bypassing the standard English-focused safety alignments and propagating harmful bias to non-English speaking communities. For spoken language technologies deployed across India's linguistically diverse population, this represents a critical failure mode. To address this cross-lingual gap, we introduce INCLUDE (Indian Cultural Lens for Understanding and Detecting Embedded Biases), a multilingual evaluation benchmark designed to quantify Indian-centric socio-cultural biases. INCLUDE consists of 2,604 prompts spanning six prompt languages: English, Hindi, Bengali, Marathi, Tamil, and Hinglish (Hindi-English code-mix). We evaluate ten open- and closed-source LLMs against this benchmark, analyzing 14,988 bias scores. Our statistical results reveal two key findings. First, Bengali yielded the highest average bias score in open-source models. Second, English demonstrated a notable reversal, producing the lowest bias in open-source models but the highest bias in closed-source models.

</details>


### [125] [Optimized Fuzzy Logic Approach with the IEEE Key Gas Method for Diagnosing Power Transformer Faults Using Dissolved Gas Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18133)
*Kim-Anh Nguyen,Huy Hoang Le,Ba Tu Phung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将模糊逻辑与IEEE特征气体法（KGM）相结合的增强模型FL-KGM，通过改进隶属函数、优化模糊规则集并创新性地分离CO和CO2气体，在变压器故障诊断中达到了98.6%的准确率，显著优于传统KGM和其他基于模糊逻辑的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的变压器故障诊断对电力系统稳定性至关重要。IEEE特征气体法（KGM）作为溶解气体分析（DGA）中广泛使用的方法，在处理模糊数据和高精度诊断方面存在局限性，需要更先进的模型来克服这些不足。

Method: FL-KGM模型将模糊逻辑与IEEE特征气体法相结合，包括：(1)引入精炼的隶属函数；(2)优化模糊规则集；(3)创新性地分离CO和CO2气体以消除诊断不一致性；(4)利用多维气体比率分析和自适应分类框架实现故障识别与分类。

Result: 基于真实数据集的实验验证表明，FL-KGM达到了高达98.6%的诊断准确率，显著优于传统KGM和其他基于模糊逻辑的方法。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明FL-KGM在推进变压器监测、实现智能故障检测和增强现代电力系统预测性维护策略方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Reliable transformer fault diagnosis is essential for maintaining power system stability. The IEEE Key Gas Method (KGM), a widely utilized approach in Dissolved Gas Analysis (DGA), exhibits limitations in addressing ambiguous data and ensuring high diagnostic accuracy. This study presents An enhanced model combining Fuzzy Logic with the IEEE Key Gas Method (FL-KGM) that introduces refined membership functions, optimized fuzzy rule sets, and a novel separation of CO and CO2 to eliminate diagnostic inconsistencies. By leveraging multidimensional gas ratio analysis and an adaptive classification framework, FL-KGM delivers superior fault identification and classification. Experimental validation utilizing real-world datasets demonstrates that FL-KGM achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, significantly outperforming KGM and other FL-based approaches. These findings elucidate the potential of FL-KGM in advancing transformer monitoring, enabling intelligent fault detection, and enhancing predictive maintenance strategies in modern power systems.

</details>


### [126] [Improving Rural Medication Safety with AI: A Scoping Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18135)
*Jeong-ah Kim,Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Daniel Terry,Maryam Rouhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过范围综述，系统检索2012至2025年多个数据库，纳入来自9个国家的12项研究，探讨人工智能技术在改善农村医疗环境患者安全、减少用药错误方面的应用与效果。结果表明AI已整合至用药管理全流程，可使处方和转录错误减少34%-80%，但面临治理框架缺失、资金限制和临床医生抵触等障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用药错误是全球医疗系统的重大威胁，导致患者伤害。农村医疗环境资源有限、专业人员不足，用药错误风险更高。目前缺乏对AI在农村医疗中减少用药错误应用情况的系统梳理，因此有必要开展范围综述，探索AI在该场景下的应用及有效性。

Method: 采用范围综述方法，系统检索2012至2025年EBSCohost、Emcare (Ovid)、MEDLINE和ProQuest消费者健康数据库等多个数据库，最终纳入来自9个国家的12项主要研究，通过主题分析法对AI在用药流程各环节的干预数据进行归纳分析。

Result: AI技术已整合到用药管理的全流程（处方、发药、用药及用药后监测）。识别出四大主题：AI类型（临床决策支持系统、机器学习、自然语言处理、智能输液泵）、受影响的用药流程阶段、减少错误和提升工作流安全的有效性、农村特有挑战（基础设施、人员培训、系统整合和警报疲劳）。基于机器学习的监测可提升事件检测能力，使处方和转录错误减少34%-80%。主要障碍包括治理框架缺失、资金限制和临床医生的抵触。

Conclusion: AI技术在农村医疗环境中具有提升药物安全的巨大潜力，能够实现数据驱动的监测、流程自动化和临床决策支持。但要充分发挥其作用，还需解决治理、资金和人员接受度等现实障碍。

Abstract: Introduction: Medication errors (MEs) represent a significant threat to global healthcare systems, contributing to patient harm. Introducing artificial intelligence (AI) in rural healthcare enhances patient safety. The aim is to explore the applications and effectiveness of AI technologies in enhancing patient safety and reducing medication errors in rural health settings.
  Methods: A scoping review was conducted through a systematic literature search spanning 2012 to 2025 across multiple databases, including EBSCohost, Emcare (Ovid), MEDLINE, and the ProQuest Consumer Health Database. Twelve primary studies from nine different nations were examined. Data were analysed thematically to obtain insights on AI interventions across the medication process.
  Results: AI technologies have been integrated into every stage of medication management, right from prescribing and dispensing to administration and post-administration monitoring. Four key themes came to light: (1) the various types of AI being utilised (like Clinical Decision Support Systems, Machine Learning, Natural Language Processing, and smart pumps); (2) the phases of the medication process that are affected; (3) how effective these technologies are in minimising errors and boosting workflow safety; and (4) rural-specific challenges including infrastructure, staff training, system integration, and alert fatigue. Several studies have demonstrated that machine learning-based surveillance improves incident detection and reduces prescribing and transcription errors by an impressive 34% to 80%. Barriers included lack of governance frameworks, financial limitations, and clinician resistance, which still present major obstacles.
  Conclusion: In rural healthcare, AI technologies hold great potential for enhancing pharmaceutical safety. They can allow data-driven monitoring, automate processes, and offer clinical decision assistance.

</details>


### [127] [FraudBench: Stress-Testing Policy-Grounded Banking Agents Against Adaptive Fraud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18136)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FraudBench，一个基于可执行银行环境的策略引导型对话代理欺诈安全基准测试。该基准构建在 τ²-bench 双控制框架与 τ-Knowledge 银行环境之上，包含 698 份内部政策文档语料库、150 个对抗性场景（107 个公开 + 43 个隐藏），测试代理在面对对话中操作身份、授权与信任的调用者时的安全行为。初步评估显示四个代理的攻击安全率仅为 49%-65%，表明现有代理在对抗性对话安全方面存在显著漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融欺诈基准仅分类静态交易或消息，通用代理安全基准则针对提示注入或一般性有害使用，均无法测试策略约束下的银行代理在调用者通过对话操纵身份、授权和信任时是否安全行事。银行场景中客服与授权、欺诈检测、政策合规密不可分，需要一个可执行的、策略驱动的基准来评估代理的对话安全能力。

Method: 基于 τ²-bench 双控制框架和 τ-Knowledge 银行环境构建 FraudBench。代理与模拟调用者通过工具在共享可变账户状态上行动；环境提供 698 文档内部政策语料库供代理检索。包含 150 个创作对抗场景，其中 107 个公开（90 个跨十种欺诈机制 + 17 个链式自适应攻击），43 个链式攻击留作隐藏集。场景标注了可观察证据、禁止行为、安全处置和干预点。安全性具有历史依赖性质：单控制任务仅缺少一个前置条件，自适应攻击使后续局部合法请求因先前试探而变不安全。

Result: 在 107 个评分任务中对四个代理进行初步单次试验评估，攻击安全率在 49%-65% 之间；最常见的跨模型弱点是钱骡（money-mule）和第一方欺诈。

Conclusion: FraudBench 填补了对话代理金融安全基准的空白，揭示了当前代理在面对多轮对话中的身份操纵、授权渐进和信任建立类攻击时安全性不足。43 个隐藏链式攻击可用于避免过拟合，为未来代理安全改进提供了可扩展的评估基础。

Abstract: Conversational agents now act for end users through tools while holding access to customer databases and internal policy documents that a caller can reach through dialogue alone. Banking is the clearest case: the same agent that answers a question can also change contact details, reset a PIN, or move money, so ordinary customer service is inseparable from authorization, fraud detection, and policy compliance. Existing financial-fraud benchmarks classify static transactions or messages, and general agent-safety benchmarks target prompt injection or generic harmful use; none test whether a policy-grounded banking agent safely acts when a caller manipulates identity, authorization, and trust over a conversation. We introduce FraudBench, an executable benchmark built on the $τ^2$-bench dual-control framework and the $τ$-Knowledge banking environment. Both the agent and the simulated caller act through tools over shared, mutable account state, and the agent may grant the caller access to selected tools; the environment exposes a 698-document internal policy corpus that the agent must retrieve from. FraudBench contains 150 authored adversarial scenarios; a frozen public set of 107 (90 across ten fraud mechanisms plus 17 chained adaptive attacks) is used for all reported runs, with 43 further chained attacks held out. Safety is history-dependent: single-control tasks satisfy every precondition but one, and adaptive attacks make a later, locally valid request unsafe because of an earlier probe, admission, or failed attempt. Each scenario is annotated with observable evidence, prohibited actions, safe dispositions, and intervention points. A preliminary single-trial evaluation of four agents on the 107 graded tasks yields attack-security between 49\% and 65\%, with money-mule and first-party fraud the most common cross-model weaknesses.

</details>


### [128] [Efficient Adaptation of LLMs for Hate Speech Detection in Low-Resource Languages: A Comparative Study on Roman Urdu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18142)
*Toneema Zubair,Muhammad Junaid Asif,Faisal Kamiran,Hafiz Hassan Saeed,Rana Fayyaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对低资源语言（罗马乌尔都语）的仇恨言论检测问题，评估了多种大型语言模型（Mistral、LLaMA、Falcon、mBERT）的零样本推理能力，并通过LoRA参数高效微调显著提升性能，F1值从0.56提升至0.93以上，同时保持了优异计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如罗马乌尔都语）缺乏标注数据、语言结构不正式且缺乏标准语法，使得仇恨言论检测极具挑战性。现有研究缺乏对这些语言中LLM性能的系统评估，需要探索高效且有效的检测方法。

Method: 在PURUTT数据集（超过72,000条标注评论）上，对Mistral、LLaMA、Falcon和mBERT四种transformer模型进行零样本推理基线评估，并与使用LoRA参数高效微调（PEFT）的表现进行对比。

Result: 零样本模型表现一般（F1=0.56），而仅更新模型少量可训练参数后，分类性能显著提升（F1>0.93）。PEFT在提供出色性能的同时保持了优秀的计算效率。

Conclusion: 参数高效微调（PEFT/LoRA）非常适合低资源语言处理任务，它能够以极低的计算成本获得接近最优的检测精度，为低资源语言的仇恨言论检测提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: It is challenging to detect hate speech in Low Resource Languages (LRLs) because of the absence of annotated data, the informality of its language structure, and the lack of standardized grammar. A good example of such a challenge is Roman Urdu which is broadly used by South Asians on social media and has a high variation while lacking contextually consistent spellings. The objective of this paper is to conduct a comprehensive assessment of Large Language Models (LLMs) for Hate Speech Detection (HSD) in Roman Urdu script and fine-tune these models using the Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) method called Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). To evaluate zero-shot inference, we benchmarked it against PEFT on different transformer models, including Mistral, LLaMA, Falcon, and multilingual BERT. Experiments are conducted on the PURUTT (Parallel Urdu and Roman Urdu Corpus for Toxic Comments and Transliteration) dataset with over 72,000 annotated comments. The results suggest that zero shot models perform moderately (F1 = 0.56), but updating a small fraction of the model trainable parameters improves the classification performance significantly (F1 > 0.93). Our results have shown that PEFT delivers outstanding performance alongside excellent computational efficiency, making it highly suitable for low-resource language processing tasks.

</details>


### [129] [RDFdL: Integrating RDF with Differential Dynamic Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18165)
*Yuyang Li,Lukas Kubelka,Julia Butte,Tobias Käfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RDFdL框架，将RDF与微分动态逻辑（dL）集成，用于表示和推理物理系统的静态知识与连续动态行为，使动态逻辑域中的安全性和可达性验证结果可转化为SPARQL查询的蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDF知识图谱擅长描述静态知识，但无法捕获或推理物理系统的动态行为（如微分方程描述的系统），这是AI驱动的信息物理系统（CPS）中的关键空白。

Method: 提出RDFdL框架：1）在RDF和SHACL中语法化表示微分方程及状态空间范围；2）通过翻译到dL提供语义；3）利用RDF与dL共享一阶逻辑基础实现集成，使dL的验证结果可转化为SPARQL查询的蕴含；4）使用Apache Jena和KeYmaera X实现流水线。

Result: 实现了使用Apache Jena进行本体驱动的RDF推理、KeYmaera X作为dL定理证明器的完整流水线，并在制造业场景中展示了其适用性。

Conclusion: RDFdL通过共享一阶逻辑基础成功架起了语义网与动态逻辑之间的桥梁，为信息物理系统提供了既能表示静态知识又能推理动态行为的统一框架。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs modeled in RDF are powerful for describing static knowledge, but they cannot capture or reason about the dynamic behavior of physical systems, e.g., systems described by differential equations, which is a critical gap for AI-driven cyber-physical systems. To solve this, we propose RDFdL, a framework that integrates RDF with Differential Dynamic Logic (dL) to represent and reason about both static knowledge and the continuous dynamics of physical systems. For the dynamic part, we syntactically represent differential equations and ranges in the state space in RDF and SHACL and provide semantics using a translation to dL. Linking RDF and dL through their shared foundation in first-order logic achieves a unique integration: verification results for safety and reachability properties in the dynamic logic domain become available as entailment to SPARQL queries over RDF data. We implement the pipeline using Apache Jena for ontology-driven RDF reasoning and KeYmaera X, the theorem prover for dL, and sketch its applicability in manufacturing.

</details>


### [130] [Adversarial Review: Structured Disagreement for Grounded Agentic Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18167)
*Eric S. Qiu,Joyce Gill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性评审（Adversarial Review, AR）协议，一种最小化的协作代码评审机制，仅需三个智能体（主编码智能体、评审者、评论者）即可在仓库级编码任务上达到甚至超越大型多智能体系统的性能，强调通过最小化、结构化和基于证据的分歧实现有效协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期多智能体LLM系统采用角色分离团队，但增加智能体数量在仓库级编码任务上收益递减；而将智能体视为被动工具（子智能体）又完全失去了智能体交互的优势。作者希望在子智能体范式中找到中间地带：最小化智能体协作，避免大型多智能体团队的复杂开销。

Method: 提出对抗性评审（AR）协议，使用三个智能体：主编码智能体负责编辑代码，评审者评估代码质量，评论者通过结构化分歧对评审进行审计，之后主智能体再根据反馈进行编辑。该协议强调分歧是最小化、结构化和基于证据的。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上，AR达到测试方法中最高的通过率，超越五智能体基线但仅用三个智能体。在SWE-PRBench上，朴素AR暴露了伪共识失败模式，但通过单一提示迭代显式添加分歧后达到最高F1。在SWE-bench Verified上也优于基线。

Conclusion: 协作式代码评审不需要大量智能体或复杂通信结构，关键在于让分歧保持最小化、结构化且基于证据。AR证明了一个极简的三智能体协作协议足以在仓库级编码任务上取得优越性能。

Abstract: Early multi-agent LLM systems often used role-separated teams, yet scaling agent count yields diminishing returns on repository-level coding tasks. Recent alternatives treat agents as passive tools (subagents), yet this removes the benefits of agent interaction entirely. We study whether a subagent paradigm can support a middle ground: minimal agentic cooperation without the overhead of large multi-agent teams. We introduce Adversarial Review (AR), a minimal cooperative code-review protocol in which a main coding agent works with a reviewer and a critic agent. The reviewer evaluates code, while the critic audits the review through structured disagreement before the main agent edits. On LiveCodeBench, AR achieves the highest pass rate among tested methods, outperforming a five-agent baseline while using only three agents. On SWE-PRBench, naive AR exposes a false-consensus failure mode, where agents converge on agreement without sufficient evidence, but a single prompt iteration that adds disagreement explicitly achieves the highest F1 among tested methods. On SWE-bench Verified, AR also shows improvements over the baselines on repository-level coding tasks. Together, AR demonstrates that cooperative code review does not require many agents or complex communication structures: it requires that disagreement be minimal, structured, and evidence-grounded.

</details>


### [131] [Looped Language Models Improve Compositional Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18171)
*Andrei Cristian Popescu,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环语言模型（looped language models）在智能体工具调用（agentic tool use）场景中的潜力，特别是在组合式工具调用（compositional tool-calling）设置下。通过在API-Bank、BFCL和NESTful三个基准上对比循环与非循环模型，发现循环计算在组合式和依赖感知的工具使用任务上普遍有益，且精度随循环深度增加而提升，但自适应推理能实现更优的计算-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型在推理基准上表现出色，但其在智能体工具使用方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。组合式工具调用需要模型协调多个API调用、维护中间状态并保持依赖关系，这对模型能力提出了更高要求，因此值得研究循环架构在此场景下的表现。

Method: 在API-Bank、BFCL和NESTful三个基准上，评估原生（native）和改造（retrofitted）的循环语言模型。将循环与非循环模型在匹配的监督微调（SFT）配方下训练，并在推理时改变循环深度（recurrent depth），同时对比固定深度与自适应推理（adaptive inference）策略。

Result: 循环计算在组合式和依赖感知的工具使用上普遍带来益处，而对孤立API调用的增益较小且与模型相关。多步工具使用的精度通常随循环深度增加而提高；自适应推理通过仅在需要时分配额外计算，实现了更优的计算-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 循环语言模型是智能体系统的有前景的架构选择，特别是在需要可靠规划、协调和执行组合式工具使用工作流的场景中。

Abstract: Looped language models have shown promising results on reasoning benchmarks, yet their potential for agentic tool use remains largely unexplored. We study this question in compositional tool-calling settings, where models must coordinate multiple API calls, maintain intermediate state, and preserve dependencies across tool interactions. We evaluate native and retrofitted looped language models on API-Bank, BFCL, and NESTful, comparing looped and non-looped models trained under matched supervised fine-tuning recipes and varying recurrent depth at inference time. In controlled experiments, recurrent computation generally benefits compositional and dependency-aware tool use, while providing smaller and more model-dependent gains on isolated API invocation. Accuracy on multi-step tool use generally increases with recurrent depth; adaptive inference, however, achieves a more favorable compute-performance trade-off by allocating additional computation only when needed. Our results suggest that looped language models are a promising architecture for agentic systems that require reliable planning, coordination, and execution of compositional tool use workflows.

</details>


### [132] [On the Triangle Inequality for the Jaccard Distance in Arbitrary Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18194)
*Costin Bădică,Amelia Bădică*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Jaccard距离在格与实值赋值上的推广，证明了在严格正、单调、模性赋值下，任意格上Jaccard距离满足三角不等式；在相对补分配格上放宽条件至正、单调、超模及对数子模赋值；并证明超模性是标准广义Jaccard距离成为度量的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于Jaccard距离满足三角不等式的结果高度依赖分配性（distributivity）等结构约束，作者希望将这些结果推广到更一般的格结构上，放宽结构性限制，从而扩大其理论适用范围。

Method: 作者从理论证明角度出发，系统地研究了Jaccard距离在一般格（arbitrary lattices）和相对补分配格（relatively complemented distributive lattices）上成立三角不等式的充分条件，并针对子模赋值调整了对称差Jaccard公式，同时通过必要的条件分析（如超模性）来刻画度量性质成立的边界。

Result: （1）在严格正、单调、模性赋值下，任意格上Jaccard距离满足三角不等式；（2）在相对补分配格上，正、单调、超模、对数子模赋值足以保证三角不等式成立；（3）对称差Jaccard形式可扩展到剖面补分配格上的子模赋值；（4）证明超模性是标准广义Jaccard距离成为度量的严格必要条件。

Conclusion: 作者放宽了Jaccard距离成立三角不等式所需的结构约束，建立了更一般化的理论框架，并将该成果映射到量子信息理论、形式概念分析与机器学习等计算领域的实际应用，同时指出尚待解决的开放数学问题。

Abstract: This paper presents new theoretical results on generalizing the Jaccard distance for lattices and real valuations. We demonstrate that when the valuation is strictly positive, monotone, and modular, the Jaccard distance satisfies the triangle inequality on arbitrary lattices, effectively generalizing earlier results that depended heavily on distributivity. Moving to relatively complemented distributive lattices (which safely drop the requirement for the global bounds found in Boolean algebras), we prove the triangle inequality holds as long as the valuation is positive, monotone, supermodular, and $\log$-submodular. Additionally, we adapt the symmetric-difference Jaccard formulation for submodular valuations to sectionally complemented distributive lattices. Shifting to necessary conditions, we prove that supermodularity is a strict requirement for the standard generalized Jaccard distance to operate as a valid metric. Finally, we map the practical value of relaxing these structural constraints to computational fields like quantum information theory, formal concept analysis, and machine learning, closing with a brief look at open mathematical problems.

</details>


### [133] [GenEx: A Graph-Based Representational Paradigm for SARS-CoV-2 Variant Detection via Codon Co-occurrence Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18238)
*Arefin Amin,Labiba Faiza Karim,M. Monir Uddin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenEx管道，将病毒基因序列（如SARS-CoV-2变体）转换为密码子共现图（MSCG和LAPCG算法），提取超过25种图特征，并利用SVD谱图特征提取（采用平方奇异值σ²代替传统特征值）来增强类别分离度，最终在23个机器学习模型上实现了对SARS-CoV-2各变体的高精度检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生物信息学方法（如序列比对、系统发育分析、突变频率统计）将基因序列视为线性字符串，采用成对密码子或核苷酸距离矩阵分析，无法捕捉基因序列中复杂的上下文相互依赖关系，因此需要一种能够建模序列结构化关联的新方法。

Method: 1) 提出GenEx管道，将原始基因序列转换为密码子共现图；2) 提出两种核心图生成与特征提取算法：MSCG（多尺度密码子共现图）和LAPCG（线性时间邻接PMI密码子图）；3) 从计算语言学的表征范式出发，将密码子序列视为结构化符号词汇表；4) 使用SVD进行谱图特征提取，并创新性地采用平方奇异值σ²替代传统特征值，以放大主次谱成分间的分离度。

Result: 训练了23个基准机器学习模型对最新的SARS-CoV-2变体（Beta、Gamma、Delta、Omicron）进行分类，在所有变体检测中取得了显著优异的结果，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: GenEx通过将基因序列转化为图结构并利用谱图特征，成功超越了传统线性序列分析方法，为病毒基因组分析提供了一种更强大的表征与分类范式。

Abstract: Genomic analysis on viruses such as SARS-CoV-2 variants: Beta, Gamma, Delta, and Omicron is heavily dominated by classical bioinformatics methods, including Sequence Alignment, Phylogenetic Analysis, and Mutation Frequency Statistics. These approaches use pairwise codon or nucleotide distance matrices to analyze gene sequences, treating them as linear strings rather than capturing their complex contextual interdependencies. We proposed GenEx, a pipeline that converts raw gene sequences into codon co-occurrence graphs and extracts more than 25 graph features. Our two most prominent techniques for graph generation and feature extraction are MSCG (Multi-Scale Codon Co-occurrence Graph) and LAPCG (Linear-time Adjacency PMI Codon Graph). Using these algorithms, we treated codon sequences as structured symbolic vocabularies interpretable to codon co-occurrence graph analysis, a representational paradigm borrowed from computational linguistics. Another major contribution includes implementing a spectral graph feature extraction using Singular Value Decomposition (SVD), using the squared singular value ($σ^2$) instead of the traditionally used eigenvalue, which helped us to amplify the separation between dominant and subdominant spectral components, thereby enhancing inter-class separability in downstream classification. And to further demonstrate that our method works, we trained 23 benchmarked ML models against the latest SARS-CoV-2 variants, achieving remarkable results in detecting all SARS-CoV-2 variants.

</details>


### [134] [Redakto - The Incognito Tab for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18260)
*Saurav Kumar Saha,Tom Röhr,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Redakto，一个开源的文本匿名化工具，可在将文本输入LLM之前去除个人身份信息（PII）。该工具提供编辑（redaction）和假名化（pseudonymization）功能，支持Web应用、REST API和MCP钩子等多种使用方式，可轻松部署在本地硬件上。作者在法律和医疗领域文本上进行了广泛的实证评估，结果表明不同匿名化策略处理后的文本在效用（utility）上与原文本相当，说明Redakto可以在不显著影响LLM任务表现的前提下进行文本匿名化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在日常应用中的普及，确保文本输入前移除个人身份信息（PII）成为一个重大挑战。欧盟新立法加剧了这一需求的紧迫性，而围绕LLM使用的隐私不确定性成为阻碍创新和研究转化的主要瓶颈。因此需要一种有效的文本匿名化工具。

Method: Redakto提供两种核心功能：PII编辑（redaction）和假名化（pseudonymization）。功能通过多种接口暴露：面向终端用户的Web应用、面向开发者和研究者的REST API以及MCP钩子。实现完全开源，计算资源需求适中，可部署在本地硬件上。作者在法律和医疗领域文本上进行了隐私性和效用性的广泛实证评估。

Result: 在法律和医疗领域的实证评估显示，使用不同匿名化策略处理后的文本在效用得分上与原文本相当，表明Redakto的匿名化可在不影响LLM任务表现的前提下使用。

Conclusion: Redakto是一个实用的开源文本匿名化工具，能在保护隐私的同时保持文本效用，适合在LLM工作流中使用，有助于解决欧盟隐私法规下的LLM部署难题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being increasingly used in everyday applications. A major challenge in the context of LLMs or Artificial Intelligence (AI) in general is to ensure privacy when using them, meaning that personally identifiable information (PII) is removed from any text that enters an LLM. These challenges have become more urgent with novel EU legislation. Uncertainty around LLM usage with respect to privacy concerns in EU countries can be a major blocker for the speed of innovation and transfer from research to applications. Here we present \textbf{Redakto}, a tool that can be used for anonymizing text prior to feeding it to an LLM or other downstream text processing. We provide state-of-the-art functionalities for both redaction of PII but also when used for pseudonymization. These functionalities are exposed such that they can easily be used by end-users, through the Redakto web application, and by developers and researchers, via REST APIs and model context protocol (MCP) hooks. The implementation is fully open source, requires modest compute resources, and can be readily deployed on local hardware. In contrast to prior work and in order to better assess the quality of the anonymized texts, we conduct extensive empirical evaluations on textual data from legal and medical domain with respect to both privacy and utility of the redacted texts. Our empirical results demonstrate that the texts anonymized with different redaction strategies achieve utility scores on par with the original texts, suggesting that anonymization with Redakto can be used for LLM tasks without substantial negative impact for the tasks we explored.

</details>


### [135] [Cacheable by Design? Training Mixture-of-Experts Routers for Locality Against the Edge Memory-Bandwidth Wall: A Pre-Registered Negative Result with a Systems Measurement Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18261)
*Shriniwas Ramesh Suram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文量化了在单张8GB GPU上服务235B参数MoE模型的"带宽墙"问题，指出解码速度受限于内存带宽而非算力。作者构建了零侵入的路由遥测工具llama-moe-trace，测量了Qwen3的路由行为，并预注册实验验证"缓存友好性"是否可训练。结果表明局部性/路由损失可将缓存未命中降低多达60%，但所有配置都无法通过预注册的<=1%困惑度门槛——未命中减少与质量紧密耦合。训练无关的缓存感知重路由与训练局部性结合能实现~80%未命中降低且困惑度<=3.4%，远优于单独使用任一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级硬件上服务大规模MoE模型时，解码受限于内存带宽而非算力，大部分专家驻留在远慢于RAM的SSD上。作者希望系统量化这一带宽墙，并探索能否通过训练使模型路由更具缓存友好性，从而降低内存带宽压力。

Method: 1) 构建字节/令牌与带宽的解析模型来量化Qwen3-235B的带宽墙；2) 开发llama-moe-trace零侵入路由遥测工具测量Qwen3-30B的路由统计特征；3) 预注册训练137M和340M MoE语言模型实验，使用辅助局部性与领域路由器损失，在"缓存未命中降低+困惑度"联合标准下评估；4) 评估训练无关的缓存感知重路由与领域预热预取等策略。

Result: Qwen3-235B在8GB GPU上实测0.44 tok/s，与带宽模型吻合；批量方案在batch 32因分页抖动崩溃。路由测量显示相邻令牌专家复用是随机的2.0倍，95%流量使用52.5%专家，13.4%专家的LRU缓存可服务66%请求。训练机制有效（未命中降低最多60%，静态固定命中率99%），但所有配置均未通过困惑度门禁。训练无关重路由可与训练局部性叠加，实现~80%未命中降低且困惑度<=3.4%。

Conclusion: 缓存未命中降低与模型质量存在紧密耦合，局部性损失的"代价"在多领域137M模型上是真实的（与StickyMoE在单领域小模型上的"近似免费"结论形成对比），且该代价随规模增大而非缩小。训练无关的缓存感知重路由是更经济的补充方案，与训练局部性叠加效果最佳。

Abstract: Serving a 235B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model on a single 8 GB GPU is bottlenecked not by compute but by memory bandwidth: decode must stream each token's active experts from whichever tier holds them, and on consumer hardware most experts sit on an SSD far slower than RAM. We quantify this bandwidth wall on Qwen3-235B (Q4_K_M, 134 GB): measured decode is 0.44 tok/s warm, matching a bytes-per-token / bandwidth model, while a batching scheme that should amortize one disk sweep instead collapses at batch 32 from paging thrash. We build llama-moe-trace, a zero-surgery router-telemetry tool, and measure routing on Qwen3-30B: adjacent-token expert reuse is 2.0x chance, 95% of traffic uses 52.5% of experts, and an LRU cache of 13.4% of experts serves 66% of requests. We then ask whether cacheability is trainable: we pre-register training of 137M MoE language models with auxiliary locality and domain router losses, under joint criteria on cache-miss reduction and perplexity. The mechanism works (misses down up to 60%; a 99% static-pin hit rate) but every configuration fails the pre-registered <=1% perplexity gate -- miss reduction and quality are tightly coupled. Concurrent StickyMoE reports the same loss as near-free on single-domain sub-25M models; on multi-domain 137M we find the tax real. Our contribution is this pre-registered, stricter-criterion, multi-domain evaluation plus edge-serving measurements. A 340M rung shows the tax does not shrink with scale (it rises slightly).
